===== README.md =====
# Introdução

O uso de animais reais na medicina veterinária é um tema debatido entre os profissionais da área. Como forma preservar o bem-estar animal, normas e alternativas foram desenvolvidas com o objetivo de reduzir e substituir o uso de animais durante atividades pedagógicas e científicas, além de aprimorar os métodos já utilizados.

Nesse contexto, o uso de simuladores didáticos são eficazes no treinamento de habilidades clínicas, contribuindo para a redução da ansiedade e permitindo a repetição ilimitada das técnicas.

O projeto de um conjunto consiste no desenvolvimento de um simulador canino de baixo custo para o estudo de ausculta pulmonar e cardíaca, utilizando de componentes relacionados a Desenvolvimento Mobile, Web e Sistemas Embarcados com Internet das Coisas (IoT).  

# Tecnologias
* C++.
* ESP-IDF

===== include/README =====

This directory is intended for project header files.

A header file is a file containing C declarations and macro definitions
to be shared between several project source files. You request the use of a
header file in your project source file (C, C++, etc) located in `src` folder
by including it, with the C preprocessing directive `#include'.

```src/main.c

#include "header.h"

int main (void)
{
 ...
}
```

Including a header file produces the same results as copying the header file
into each source file that needs it. Such copying would be time-consuming
and error-prone. With a header file, the related declarations appear
in only one place. If they need to be changed, they can be changed in one
place, and programs that include the header file will automatically use the
new version when next recompiled. The header file eliminates the labor of
finding and changing all the copies as well as the risk that a failure to
find one copy will result in inconsistencies within a program.

In C, the convention is to give header files names that end with `.h'.

Read more about using header files in official GCC documentation:

* Include Syntax
* Include Operation
* Once-Only Headers
* Computed Includes

https://gcc.gnu.org/onlinedocs/cpp/Header-Files.html

#include "application/hardware/dto/WifiConnectDto.hpp"
class WifiStorage {
public:
  ~WifiStorage() = default;

  virtual bool save(const WifiConnectDto &dto) = 0;
  virtual bool load(WifiConnectDto &dto) = 0;
  virtual bool clear() = 0;
};

===== include/application/hardware/dto/WifiConnectDto.hpp =====
#pragma once

#include <string>

struct WifiConnectDto {
  const std::string ssid;
  const std::string password;
  const std::string ip;
  const std::string gateway;
  const std::string subnet;

  bool isDHCP() const { return ip == ""; }
};

===== include/application/hardware/dto/WifiScanDto.hpp =====
#pragma once

#include <cstdint>
#include <string>

enum class WifiStatus {
  WIFI_NONE = 0,
  WIFI_CONNECTING,
  WIFI_CONNECTED,
  WIFI_ERROR
};

struct WifiScanDto {
  std::string ssid;
  bool secure{false};
  WifiStatus status{WifiStatus::WIFI_NONE};
  int32_t rssi{0};
};

===== include/application/service/DeviceRegister.hpp =====
#pragma once

#include "application/hardware/WifiAPObserver.hpp"
#include "application/hardware/WifiSTAObserver.hpp"
#include "application/security/DeviceKeys.hpp"
#include "application/security/MqttClient.hpp"
#include "application/service/Authenticator.hpp"
#include "application/service/DeviceRegisterObserver.hpp"
#include "application/service/TimeSync.hpp"
#include <atomic>
#include <cstdint>
#include <freertos/FreeRTOS.h>
#include <freertos/semphr.h>
#include <freertos/task.h>
#include <mutex>
#include <string>
#include <vector>

class DeviceRegister : public WifiSTAObserver, public WifiAPObserver {
public:
  DeviceRegister(DeviceKeys &keys, MqttClient &mqtt, Authenticator &auth,
                 TimeSync &timeSync, std::string mac);
  ~DeviceRegister() override;

  void begin();
  void registerObserver(DeviceRegisterObserver *observer);
  void unregisterObserver(DeviceRegisterObserver *observer);

  // WifiSTAObserver
  void onConnect(const WifiScanDto &connectDto) override;
  void onDisconnect(const WifiScanDto &connectDto) override;
  void onError(const WifiScanDto &connectDto) override;

  // WifiAPObserver
  void onConnect() override;
  void onDisconnect() override;

private:
  static void taskEntry(void *param);
  void run();
  // Kicks the round when out of configuration mode.
  void evaluateGate();
  void registerDevice();
  void failRegistration();
  void retryLater(bool generated, const char *reason);
  bool waitForAck(std::string &out, uint32_t timeoutMs);
  void onMqttMessage(const std::string &topic, const std::string &payload);
  void logAccessToken();

  DeviceKeys &keys;
  MqttClient &mqtt;
  Authenticator &auth;
  TimeSync &timeSync;
  const std::string mac;

  // Written only from the Wi-Fi event task, read by evaluateGate().
  bool staConnected{false};
  uint8_t apClients{0};

  std::vector<DeviceRegisterObserver *> observers;
  TaskHandle_t task{nullptr};
  SemaphoreHandle_t ackSem{nullptr};
  std::mutex ackMutex;
  std::string ackPayload;
  std::atomic<bool> waiting{false};
  bool tokenLogged{false};
};

===== include/application/service/DeviceRegisterObserver.hpp =====
#pragma once

#include <string>

class DeviceRegisterObserver {
public:
  virtual ~DeviceRegisterObserver() = default;

  // The MQTT round is starting: the radio is needed for TLS.
  virtual void onRegistering() {}
  virtual void onGeneratingKey(const std::string &mac) {}
  virtual void onKeyGenerationFailed() {}
  virtual void onKeyLoaded() {}
  virtual void onRegistered(const std::string &status,
                            const std::string &deviceId) {}
  virtual void onRejected(const std::string &message, bool preProvisioned) {}
  virtual void onRetryLater(const std::string &reason, bool keepingKey) {}
  virtual void onKeySaved() {}
  virtual void onKeySaveFailed() {}
  virtual void onTokenIssued(const std::string &token) {}
  virtual void onClockNotSynced() {}
};

===== include/application/service/ScanService.hpp =====
#pragma once

#include "application/hardware/WifiAPObserver.hpp"
#include "application/hardware/WifiSTA.hpp"
#include "application/hardware/WifiSTAObserver.hpp"
#include "application/service/DeviceRegisterObserver.hpp"
#include <cstdint>
#include <vector>

// Scan policy:
// - scans from boot until the device connects to a Wi-Fi network (STA);
// - keeps scanning while a client is on the setup AP, even with the STA
//   connected, because a client on the portal probably wants to change
//   networks; stops again once the client leaves;
// - resumes whenever the STA disconnects or fails to connect;
// - pauses while the device registration talks to the broker: the TLS
//   handshake needs the radio, and a concurrent scan stretches it past
//   the connect timeout.
//
// The ESP32 has a single radio, so the scan task paces its cycles (short
// per-channel dwell + pause between cycles) to leave the SoftAP enough
// airtime for beacons and data while someone is browsing the portal.
class ScanService : public WifiAPObserver,
                    public WifiSTAObserver,
                    public DeviceRegisterObserver {
public:
  explicit ScanService(WifiSTA &wifiSTA) : wifiSTA(wifiSTA) {}

  // Applies the scan policy for the current state (called once at boot).
  void begin();

  // WifiAPObserver
  void onConnect() override;
  void onDisconnect() override;

  // WifiSTAObserver
  void onConnect(const WifiScanDto &connectDto) override;
  void onConnecting(const WifiScanDto &connectDto) override;
  void onError(const WifiScanDto &connectDto) override;
  void onDisconnect(const WifiScanDto &connectDto) override;
  void onScan(const std::vector<WifiScanDto> &scanList) override;

  // DeviceRegisterObserver
  void onRegistering() override;
  void onRegistered(const std::string &status,
                    const std::string &deviceId) override;
  void onRejected(const std::string &message, bool preProvisioned) override;
  void onRetryLater(const std::string &reason, bool keepingKey) override;
  void onKeyGenerationFailed() override;

private:
  void updateScan();

  uint8_t apClients{0};
  bool staConnected{false};
  WifiSTA &wifiSTA;
};

#pragma once

// Starts/refreshes the network time synchronization; asynchronous and
// idempotent. The device JWT `exp` claim requires a synced clock.
class TimeSync {
public:
  virtual ~TimeSync() = default;

  virtual void sync() = 0;
};

===== include/application/security/MqttClient.hpp =====
#pragma once

#include "application/security/MqttObserver.hpp"
#include <functional>
#include <string>

class MqttClient {
public:
  using MessageHandler =
      std::function<void(const std::string &topic, const std::string &payload)>;

  virtual ~MqttClient() = default;

  virtual void onMessage(MessageHandler handler) = 0;
  virtual bool connect(uint32_t timeoutMs) = 0;
  virtual void disconnect() = 0;
  virtual bool subscribe(const std::string &topic) = 0;
  virtual bool publish(const std::string &topic,
                       const std::string &payload) = 0;

  virtual void registerObserver(MqttObserver *observer) = 0;
  virtual void unregisterObserver(MqttObserver *observer) = 0;
};

};

===== include/infra/hardware/EspWifiAP.hpp =====
#pragma once

#include <DNSServer.h>
#include <WiFi.h>
#include <WiFiAP.h>
#include <freertos/FreeRTOS.h>
#include <freertos/task.h>
#include <mutex>
#include <vector>

#include "application/hardware/WifiAP.hpp"
#include "application/hardware/WifiAPObserver.hpp"

class EspWifiAP : public WifiAP {
public:
  EspWifiAP();
  ~EspWifiAP() override;
  void start() override;
  void stop() override;
  void registerObserver(WifiAPObserver *observer) override;
  void unregisterObserver(WifiAPObserver *observer) override;

private:
  TaskHandle_t dnsLoop;

  bool running{false};
  std::vector<WifiAPObserver *> observers;
  std::mutex observersMutex;
  DNSServer dns;
  const byte DNS_PORT{53};
  uint16_t countConnect{0};

  IPAddress apIP{192, 168, 4, 1};
  IPAddress gateway{192, 168, 4, 1};
  IPAddress subnet{255, 255, 255, 0};

  std::vector<WifiAPObserver *> lockedObservers();

  void onWifiEvent(WiFiEvent_t event);
};

===== include/infra/hardware/EspWifiSTA.hpp =====
#pragma once

#include "application/hardware/WifiSTA.hpp"
#include "application/hardware/WifiSTAObserver.hpp"
#include "application/hardware/dto/WifiScanDto.hpp"
#include <WiFi.h>
#include <atomic>
#include <freertos/FreeRTOS.h>
#include <freertos/task.h>
#include <mutex>
#include <vector>

class EspWifiSTA : public WifiSTA {
public:
  EspWifiSTA();
  ~EspWifiSTA() override;
  void setup() override;
  void connect(const WifiConnectDto &connectDto) override;
  void disconnect() override;
  bool isConnected() override;
  bool isScanning() override;
  void startScan() override;
  void stopScan() override;

  void registerObserver(WifiSTAObserver *observer) override;
  void unregisterObserver(WifiSTAObserver *observer) override;

private:
  // Shared between the WiFi event task, the scan task and the proxy task:
  // every access goes through the corresponding mutex.
  std::vector<WifiSTAObserver *> observers;
  std::mutex observersMutex;
  std::atomic<bool> scanning{false};
  std::mutex scanMutex;
  TaskHandle_t scanTask{nullptr};
  WifiScanDto statusNetwork;
  std::mutex statusMutex;

  void connectDHCP(const WifiConnectDto &connectCmd);
  void connectStatic(const WifiConnectDto &connectCmd);
  void connect(const std::string &ssid, const std::string &pass);
  void processScan();
  WifiScanDto processScanItem(int i);
  void onWifiEvent(WiFiEvent_t event, WiFiEventInfo_t info);

  WifiScanDto snapshotStatus();
  WifiScanDto setErrorStatus();
  std::vector<WifiSTAObserver *> lockedObservers();
};

===== include/infra/hardware/WifiSTAProxy.hpp =====
#pragma once

#include "application/hardware/WifiSTA.hpp"
#include <freertos/FreeRTOS.h>
#include <freertos/queue.h>
#include <freertos/task.h>

class WifiSTAProxy : public WifiSTA {
public:
  explicit WifiSTAProxy(WifiSTA &sta);
  ~WifiSTAProxy() override;

  void setup() override;
  void connect(const WifiConnectDto &connectDto) override;
  void disconnect() override;
  bool isConnected() override;
  bool isScanning() override;
  void startScan() override;
  void stopScan() override;

  void registerObserver(WifiSTAObserver *observer) override;
  void unregisterObserver(WifiSTAObserver *observer) override;

private:
  enum class CommandType { Connect, Disconnect, StartScan, StopScan };

  struct Command {
    CommandType type;
    WifiConnectDto *connectDto;
  };

  WifiSTA &sta;
  QueueHandle_t queue{nullptr};
  TaskHandle_t task{nullptr};

  static void taskEntry(void *param);
  bool enqueue(CommandType type, WifiConnectDto *connectDto = nullptr);
  void execute(const Command &command);
};

===== include/infra/hardware/EspMacAddress.hpp =====
#pragma once

#include <string>

// Hardware identity: the eFuse MAC burned into the chip, formatted as the
// platform contract expects (uppercase with colons, AA:BB:CC:DD:EE:FF).
// Unlike the Wi-Fi interface MAC it never changes and does not depend on the
// Wi-Fi driver being started; it also matches the MAC printed on the board.
namespace EspMacAddress {
std::string get();
}

#pragma once

#include "application/service/DeviceRegisterObserver.hpp"

class DeviceRegisterLogger : public DeviceRegisterObserver {
public:
  void onGeneratingKey(const std::string &mac) override;
  void onKeyGenerationFailed() override;
  void onKeyLoaded() override;
  void onRegistered(const std::string &status,
                    const std::string &deviceId) override;
  void onRejected(const std::string &message, bool preProvisioned) override;
  void onRetryLater(const std::string &reason, bool keepingKey) override;
  void onKeySaved() override;
  void onKeySaveFailed() override;
  void onTokenIssued(const std::string &token) override;
  void onClockNotSynced() override;
};

// Injected by extra/getenv.py from .env / system environment; the fallbacks

===== lib/README =====

This directory is intended for project specific (private) libraries.
PlatformIO will compile them to static libraries and link into the executable file.

The source code of each library should be placed in a separate directory
("lib/your_library_name/[Code]").

For example, see the structure of the following example libraries `Foo` and `Bar`:

|--lib
|  |
|  |--Bar
|  |  |--docs
|  |  |--examples
|  |  |--src
|  |     |- Bar.c
|  |     |- Bar.h
|  |  |- library.json (optional. for custom build options, etc) https://docs.platformio.org/page/librarymanager/config.html
|  |
|  |--Foo
|  |  |- Foo.c
|  |  |- Foo.h
|  |
|  |- README --> THIS FILE
|
|- platformio.ini
|--src
   |- main.c

Example contents of `src/main.c` using Foo and Bar:
```
#include <Foo.h>
#include <Bar.h>

int main (void)
{
  ...
}

```

The PlatformIO Library Dependency Finder will find automatically dependent
libraries by scanning project source files.

More information about PlatformIO Library Dependency Finder
- https://docs.platformio.org/page/librarymanager/ldf.html

===== src/application/service/DeviceRegister.cpp =====
#include "application/service/DeviceRegister.hpp"
#include "application/security/JwtBuilder.hpp"
#include <ArduinoJson.h>
#include <cctype>
#include <algorithm>
#include <time.h>

// Injected by extra/getenv.py from .env / system environment; the fallbacks
// follow the platform defaults (device-api.md section 2.1).
#ifndef MQTT_REGISTRATION_TOPIC
#define MQTT_REGISTRATION_TOPIC "register"
#endif
#ifndef MQTT_ACK_TOPIC
#define MQTT_ACK_TOPIC "register/ack/{mac}"
#endif

namespace {

constexpr char CANONICAL_PREFIX[] = "echoes-device-registration-v1";
// 2020-01-01: minimum sane clock, the JWT `exp` claim needs SNTP first.
constexpr time_t MIN_VALID_TIME = 1577836800;

std::string trimmed(std::string s) {
  const auto isSpace = [](unsigned char c) { return std::isspace(c) != 0; };
  while (!s.empty() && isSpace(static_cast<unsigned char>(s.front()))) {
    s.erase(s.begin());
  }
  while (!s.empty() && isSpace(static_cast<unsigned char>(s.back()))) {
    s.pop_back();
  }
  return s;
}

std::string ackTopicFor(const std::string &mac) {
  std::string topic(MQTT_ACK_TOPIC);
  const std::string placeholder = "{mac}";
  const size_t pos = topic.find(placeholder);
  if (pos != std::string::npos) {
    topic.replace(pos, placeholder.size(), mac);
  }
  return topic;
}

} // namespace

DeviceRegister::DeviceRegister(DeviceKeys &keys, MqttClient &mqtt,
                                Authenticator &auth, TimeSync &timeSync,
                                std::string mac)
    : keys(keys), mqtt(mqtt), auth(auth), timeSync(timeSync),
      mac(std::move(mac)) {}

DeviceRegister::~DeviceRegister() {
  if (task != nullptr) {
    vTaskDelete(task);
    task = nullptr;
  }
  if (ackSem != nullptr) {
    vSemaphoreDelete(ackSem);
    ackSem = nullptr;
  }
}

void DeviceRegister::begin() {
  if (ackSem == nullptr) {
    ackSem = xSemaphoreCreateBinary();
  }
  if (task == nullptr) {
    xTaskCreate(taskEntry, "device-registrar", 8192, this, 2, &task);
  }
  mqtt.onMessage([this](const std::string &topic, const std::string &payload) {
    this->onMqttMessage(topic, payload);
  });
}

void DeviceRegister::registerObserver(DeviceRegisterObserver *observer) {
  observers.push_back(observer);
}

void DeviceRegister::unregisterObserver(DeviceRegisterObserver *observer) {
  observers.erase(std::remove(observers.begin(), observers.end(), observer),
                  observers.end());
}

void DeviceRegister::onConnect(const WifiScanDto & /*connectDto*/) {
  staConnected = true;
  evaluateGate();
}

void DeviceRegister::onDisconnect(const WifiScanDto & /*connectDto*/) {
  staConnected = false;
}

void DeviceRegister::onError(const WifiScanDto & /*connectDto*/) {
  staConnected = false;
}

void DeviceRegister::onConnect() {
  // Configuration mode: platform actions are deferred.
  apClients++;
}

void DeviceRegister::onDisconnect() {
  if (apClients > 0) {
    apClients--;
  }
  evaluateGate();
}

void DeviceRegister::evaluateGate() {
  if (staConnected && apClients == 0 && task != nullptr) {
    xTaskNotifyGive(task);
  }
}

void DeviceRegister::taskEntry(void *param) {
  static_cast<DeviceRegister *>(param)->run();
}

void DeviceRegister::run() {
  while (true) {
    ulTaskNotifyTake(pdTRUE, portMAX_DELAY);

    // Out of configuration mode: the radio is quiet for the whole round —
    // clock sync, TLS registration and the access token.
    for (auto *o : observers) {
      o->onRegistering();
    }
    timeSync.sync();
    registerDevice();

    if (keys.isLoaded()) {
      logAccessToken();
    }
  }
}

void DeviceRegister::registerDevice() {
  // Prefer the pre-provisioned/stored key; generate one only when absent.
  const bool generated = !keys.load();
  if (generated) {
    for (auto *o : observers) {
      o->onGeneratingKey(mac);
    }
    if (!keys.create()) {
      for (auto *o : observers) {
        o->onKeyGenerationFailed();
      }
      return;
    }
  } else {
    for (auto *o : observers) {
      o->onKeyLoaded();
    }
  }

  const std::string publicKey = trimmed(keys.publicKeyPem());

  if (!mqtt.connect(20000)) {
    retryLater(generated, "MQTT broker unreachable");
    return;
  }

  // Canonical message signed by the device (device-api.md section 2.3).
  const std::string message =
      std::string(CANONICAL_PREFIX) + "\n" + mac + "\n" + publicKey;
  std::string der;
  if (!keys.sign(message, der)) {
    retryLater(generated, "failed to sign the registration message");
    return;
  }

  JsonDocument doc;
  doc["mac"] = mac;
  doc["public_key"] = publicKey;
  // base64url of the DER signature is accepted (section 2.4).
  doc["signature"] = JwtBuilder::base64Url(der);
  std::string payload;
  serializeJson(doc, payload);

  {
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(ackMutex);
    ackPayload.clear();
    waiting = true;
  }
  xSemaphoreTake(ackSem, 0); // drain a stale ack from a previous attempt

  if (!mqtt.subscribe(ackTopicFor(mac)) ||
      !mqtt.publish(MQTT_REGISTRATION_TOPIC, payload)) {
    retryLater(generated, "MQTT subscribe/publish failed");
    return;
  }

  std::string ack;
  if (!waitForAck(ack, 15000)) {
    retryLater(generated, "no registration ack (is the platform online?)");
    return;
  }

  JsonDocument ackDoc;
  if (deserializeJson(ackDoc, ack) != DeserializationError::Ok) {
    retryLater(generated, "malformed registration ack");
    return;
  }

  const std::string status = ackDoc["status"] | "";
  const std::string deviceId = ackDoc["deviceId"] | "";

  if (status == "REGISTERED" || status == "ALREADY_REGISTERED") {
    if (generated && !keys.store()) {
      // Without the file the next boot would register a NEW key, and the
      // platform never overwrites a MAC: keep the key in RAM for this boot.
      for (auto *o : observers) {
        o->onKeySaveFailed();
      }
    } else if (generated) {
      for (auto *o : observers) {
        o->onKeySaved();
      }
    }
    for (auto *o : observers) {
      o->onRegistered(status, deviceId);
    }
    mqtt.disconnect();
    return;
  }

  for (auto *o : observers) {
    o->onRejected(ackDoc["message"] | "", !generated);
  }
  // A generated key was never stored; a pre-provisioned one stays on flash
  // (only the in-memory copy is dropped, it is reloaded on the next wake).
  failRegistration();
}

void DeviceRegister::failRegistration() {
  mqtt.disconnect();
  keys.reset();
}

void DeviceRegister::retryLater(bool generated, const char *reason) {
  for (auto *o : observers) {
    o->onRetryLater(reason, !generated);
  }
  if (generated) {
    // Nothing was confirmed and nothing was stored: discard and retry.
    failRegistration();
  } else {
    // The pre-provisioned key is presumably already known by the platform;
    // keep it usable and let a later connection confirm.
    mqtt.disconnect();
  }
}

bool DeviceRegister::waitForAck(std::string &out, uint32_t timeoutMs) {
  if (xSemaphoreTake(ackSem, pdMS_TO_TICKS(timeoutMs)) != pdTRUE) {
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(ackMutex);
    waiting = false;
    return false;
  }
  std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(ackMutex);
  waiting = false;
  out = ackPayload;
  ackPayload.clear();
  return true;
}

void DeviceRegister::onMqttMessage(const std::string &topic,
                                   const std::string &payload) {
  if (!waiting || topic != ackTopicFor(mac)) {
    return;
  }
  {
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(ackMutex);
    ackPayload = payload;
  }
  xSemaphoreGive(ackSem);
}

void DeviceRegister::logAccessToken() {
  if (tokenLogged) {
    return;
  }
  // The JWT `exp` claim needs a synced clock; give SNTP a moment.
  for (int i = 0; i < 100 && time(nullptr) < MIN_VALID_TIME; i++) {
    vTaskDelay(pdMS_TO_TICKS(100));
  }

  const std::string token = auth.issueToken();
  if (token.empty()) {
    for (auto *o : observers) {
      o->onClockNotSynced();
    }
    return;
  }
  tokenLogged = true;
  for (auto *o : observers) {
    o->onTokenIssued(token);
  }
}

===== src/application/service/ScanService.cpp =====
#include "application/service/ScanService.hpp"

void ScanService::begin() { updateScan(); }

void ScanService::onConnect() {
  apClients++;
  updateScan();
}

void ScanService::onDisconnect() {
  if (apClients > 0) {
    apClients--;
  }
  updateScan();
}

void ScanService::onConnect(const WifiScanDto & /*connectDto*/) {
  staConnected = true;
  updateScan();
}

void ScanService::onConnecting(const WifiScanDto & /*connectDto*/) {
  // EspWifiSTA::connect() already stops the scan itself.
}

void ScanService::onError(const WifiScanDto & /*connectDto*/) {
  staConnected = false;
  updateScan();
}

void ScanService::onDisconnect(const WifiScanDto & /*connectDto*/) {
  staConnected = false;
  updateScan();
}

void ScanService::onScan(const std::vector<WifiScanDto> & /*scanList*/) {
  // Consumed by the web controller.
}

void ScanService::onRegistering() {
  // The TLS handshake needs the radio quiet; updateScan() re-applies the
  // policy when the registration round ends.
  wifiSTA.stopScan();
}

void ScanService::onRegistered(const std::string & /*status*/,
                               const std::string & /*deviceId*/) {
  updateScan();
}

void ScanService::onRejected(const std::string & /*message*/,
                             bool /*preProvisioned*/) {
  updateScan();
}

void ScanService::onRetryLater(const std::string & /*reason*/,
                               bool /*keepingKey*/) {
  updateScan();
}

void ScanService::onKeyGenerationFailed() { updateScan(); }

void ScanService::updateScan() {
  // A client on the setup AP probably wants to change networks, so keep
  // the list fresh even with the STA connected.
  const bool shouldScan = !staConnected || apClients > 0;
  if (shouldScan) {
    wifiSTA.startScan();
  } else {
    wifiSTA.stopScan();
  }
}

===== src/application/security/JwtBuilder.cpp =====
#include "application/security/JwtBuilder.hpp"
#include <cstring>

namespace JwtBuilder {
namespace {

const char kTable[] =
    "ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZabcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz0123456789-_";

} // namespace

std::string base64Url(const uint8_t *data, size_t len) {
  std::string out;
  out.reserve((len / 3 + 1) * 4);
  size_t i = 0;
  while (i + 3 <= len) {
    const uint32_t v =
        (uint32_t(data[i]) << 16) | (uint32_t(data[i + 1]) << 8) | data[i + 2];
    out += kTable[(v >> 18) & 0x3F];
    out += kTable[(v >> 12) & 0x3F];
    out += kTable[(v >> 6) & 0x3F];
    out += kTable[v & 0x3F];
    i += 3;
  }
  if (len - i == 1) {
    const uint32_t v = uint32_t(data[i]) << 16;
    out += kTable[(v >> 18) & 0x3F];
    out += kTable[(v >> 12) & 0x3F];
  } else if (len - i == 2) {
    const uint32_t v = (uint32_t(data[i]) << 16) | (uint32_t(data[i + 1]) << 8);
    out += kTable[(v >> 18) & 0x3F];
    out += kTable[(v >> 12) & 0x3F];
    out += kTable[(v >> 6) & 0x3F];
  }
  return out;
}

std::string base64Url(const std::string &data) {
  return base64Url(reinterpret_cast<const uint8_t *>(data.data()), data.size());
}

std::string derToJose(const std::string &der) {
  // Ecdsa-Sig-Value ::= SEQUENCE { r INTEGER, s INTEGER }
  const uint8_t *p = reinterpret_cast<const uint8_t *>(der.data());
  const size_t len = der.size();
  if (len < 8 || len > 72 || p[0] != 0x30) {
    return {};
  }
  if (p[1] != len - 2) { // P-256 signatures always use short-form lengths
    return {};
  }

  size_t pos = 2;
  uint8_t out[64] = {0};
  for (int part = 0; part < 2; part++) {
    if (pos + 2 > len || p[pos] != 0x02) {
      return {};
    }
    const size_t intLen = p[pos + 1];
    pos += 2;
    if (intLen == 0 || intLen > 33 || pos + intLen > len) {
      return {};
    }

    const uint8_t *src = p + pos;
    size_t n = intLen;
    if (n == 33) { // leading zero byte that keeps the INTEGER positive
      if (src[0] != 0x00) {
        return {};
      }
      src++;
      n--;
    }
    while (n > 0 && src[0] == 0x00) { // DER strips insignificant zeros
      src++;
      n--;
    }
    if (n > 32) {
      return {};
    }
    std::memcpy(out + 32 * part + (32 - n), src, n);
    pos += intLen;
  }

  return std::string(reinterpret_cast<const char *>(out), 64);
}

} // namespace JwtBuilder

===== src/infra/hardware/EspWifiAP.cpp =====
#include "infra/hardware/EspWifiAP.hpp"
#include <algorithm>

EspWifiAP::EspWifiAP() {
  WiFi.onEvent([this](WiFiEvent_t event, WiFiEventInfo_t /*info*/) {
    this->onWifiEvent(event);
  });
  xTaskCreate(
      [](void *task) {
        auto *wifiAp = static_cast<EspWifiAP *>(task);
        while (true) {
          if (wifiAp->running) {
            wifiAp->dns.processNextRequest();
          }
          vTaskDelay(pdMS_TO_TICKS(10));
        }
      },
      "dns-loop", 4096, this, 1, &dnsLoop);
}

EspWifiAP::~EspWifiAP() {
  stop();
  if (dnsLoop != nullptr) {
    vTaskDelete(dnsLoop);
    dnsLoop = nullptr;
  }
}

void EspWifiAP::start() {
  if (running) {
    return;
  }

  WiFi.softAPConfig(apIP, gateway, subnet);
  WiFi.softAP("Echoes Setup", "echoes123456");
  dns.start(DNS_PORT, "*", apIP);

  running = true;
  vTaskResume(dnsLoop);
}

void EspWifiAP::stop() {
  if (!running) {
    return;
  }

  WiFi.softAPdisconnect(true);
  dns.stop();
  running = false;
  vTaskSuspend(dnsLoop);
}

void EspWifiAP::registerObserver(WifiAPObserver *observer) {
  std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(observersMutex);
  observers.push_back(observer);
}

void EspWifiAP::unregisterObserver(WifiAPObserver *observer) {
  std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(observersMutex);
  observers.erase(std::remove(observers.begin(), observers.end(), observer),
                  observers.end());
}

std::vector<WifiAPObserver *> EspWifiAP::lockedObservers() {
  std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(observersMutex);
  return observers;
}

void EspWifiAP::onWifiEvent(WiFiEvent_t event) {
  const auto currentObservers = lockedObservers();
  switch (event) {
  case ARDUINO_EVENT_WIFI_AP_STACONNECTED:
    countConnect++;
    for (auto &obs : currentObservers)
      obs->onConnect();
    break;

  case ARDUINO_EVENT_WIFI_AP_STADISCONNECTED:
    // Only notify a disconnect when a client was actually connected,
    // otherwise observers that count connections get out of sync.
    if (countConnect > 0) {
      countConnect--;
      for (auto &obs : currentObservers)
        obs->onDisconnect();
    }
    break;
  default:
    break;
  }
}

===== src/infra/hardware/WifiSTAProxy.cpp =====
#include "infra/hardware/WifiSTAProxy.hpp"

WifiSTAProxy::WifiSTAProxy(WifiSTA &sta) : sta(sta) {
  queue = xQueueCreate(8, sizeof(Command));
  if (queue == nullptr) {
    return;
  }
  if (xTaskCreate(taskEntry, "wifi-sta-proxy", 4096, this, 2, &task) != pdPASS) {
    vQueueDelete(queue);
    queue = nullptr;
    task = nullptr;
  }
}

WifiSTAProxy::~WifiSTAProxy() {
  if (task != nullptr) {
    vTaskDelete(task);
    task = nullptr;
  }
  if (queue != nullptr) {
    Command command{};
    while (xQueueReceive(queue, &command, 0) == pdTRUE) {
      delete command.connectDto;
    }
    vQueueDelete(queue);
    queue = nullptr;
  }
}

void WifiSTAProxy::setup() { sta.setup(); }

void WifiSTAProxy::connect(const WifiConnectDto &connectDto) {
  enqueue(CommandType::Connect, new WifiConnectDto(connectDto));
}

void WifiSTAProxy::disconnect() { enqueue(CommandType::Disconnect); }

bool WifiSTAProxy::isConnected() { return sta.isConnected(); }

bool WifiSTAProxy::isScanning() { return sta.isScanning(); }

void WifiSTAProxy::startScan() { enqueue(CommandType::StartScan); }

void WifiSTAProxy::stopScan() { enqueue(CommandType::StopScan); }

void WifiSTAProxy::registerObserver(WifiSTAObserver *observer) {
  sta.registerObserver(observer);
}

void WifiSTAProxy::unregisterObserver(WifiSTAObserver *observer) {
  sta.unregisterObserver(observer);
}

void WifiSTAProxy::taskEntry(void *param) {
  auto *proxy = static_cast<WifiSTAProxy *>(param);
  Command command{};
  while (true) {
    if (xQueueReceive(proxy->queue, &command, portMAX_DELAY) == pdTRUE) {
      proxy->execute(command);
    }
  }
}

bool WifiSTAProxy::enqueue(CommandType type, WifiConnectDto *connectDto) {
  if (queue == nullptr) {
    delete connectDto;
    return false;
  }

  Command command{type, connectDto};
  if (xQueueSend(queue, &command, 0) != pdTRUE) {
    delete connectDto;
    return false;
  }
  return true;
}

void WifiSTAProxy::execute(const Command &command) {
  switch (command.type) {
  case CommandType::Connect:
    if (command.connectDto != nullptr) {
      sta.connect(*command.connectDto);
      delete command.connectDto;
    }
    break;
  case CommandType::Disconnect:
    sta.disconnect();
    break;
  case CommandType::StartScan:
    sta.startScan();
    break;
  case CommandType::StopScan:
    sta.stopScan();
    break;
  }
}

===== src/infra/hardware/EspMacAddress.cpp =====
#include "infra/hardware/EspMacAddress.hpp"
#include <cstdio>
#include <esp_mac.h>

namespace {

std::string format(const uint8_t mac[6]) {
  char buf[18];
  snprintf(buf, sizeof buf, "%02X:%02X:%02X:%02X:%02X:%02X", mac[0], mac[1],
           mac[2], mac[3], mac[4], mac[5]);
  return std::string(buf);
}

} // namespace

std::string EspMacAddress::get() {
  uint8_t mac[6];
  esp_efuse_mac_get_default(mac);
  return format(mac);
}

===== src/infra/hardware/EspWifiSTA.cpp =====
#include "infra/hardware/EspWifiSTA.hpp"
#include <algorithm>
#include <esp_wifi.h>
#include <vector>

EspWifiSTA::EspWifiSTA() {
  xTaskCreate(
      [](void *param) {
        auto *wifiSTA = static_cast<EspWifiSTA *>(param);
        while (true) {
          if (wifiSTA->scanning) {
            wifiSTA->processScan();
            // Give the SoftAP its airtime back between scan cycles; a
            // back-to-back scan starves the AP beacons and drops clients.
            vTaskDelay(pdMS_TO_TICKS(3000));
          } else {
            vTaskDelay(pdMS_TO_TICKS(250));
          }
        }
      },
      "scan-task", 4096, this, 1, &scanTask);
}

EspWifiSTA::~EspWifiSTA() {
  disconnect();
  if (scanTask != nullptr) {
    vTaskDelete(scanTask);
    scanTask = nullptr;
  }
}

void EspWifiSTA::setup() {
  WiFi.onEvent([this](WiFiEvent_t event, WiFiEventInfo_t info) {
    this->onWifiEvent(event, info);
  });
  WiFi.mode(WIFI_AP_STA);
}

void EspWifiSTA::connect(const WifiConnectDto &connectDto) {
  stopScan();

  WifiScanDto status;
  {
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(statusMutex);
    statusNetwork.ssid = connectDto.ssid;
    statusNetwork.status = WifiStatus::WIFI_CONNECTING;
    statusNetwork.secure = !connectDto.password.empty();
    statusNetwork.rssi = 0;
    status = statusNetwork;
  }
  for (auto *o : lockedObservers()) {
    o->onConnecting(status);
  }

  if (connectDto.isDHCP())
    connectDHCP(connectDto);
  else
    connectStatic(connectDto);
}

void EspWifiSTA::connectDHCP(const WifiConnectDto &connectCmd) {
  WiFi.config(IPAddress(0, 0, 0, 0), IPAddress(0, 0, 0, 0),
              IPAddress(0, 0, 0, 0));
  connect(connectCmd.ssid, connectCmd.password);
}

void EspWifiSTA::connectStatic(const WifiConnectDto &connectCmd) {
  IPAddress ipParsed, gatewayParsed, subnetParsed;
  IPAddress dns1(8, 8, 8, 8);
  IPAddress dns2(8, 8, 4, 4);

  auto failParseIps = !ipParsed.fromString(connectCmd.ip.c_str()) ||
                      !gatewayParsed.fromString(connectCmd.gateway.c_str()) ||
                      !subnetParsed.fromString(connectCmd.subnet.c_str());

  if (failParseIps) {
    const WifiScanDto status = setErrorStatus();
    for (auto *o : lockedObservers()) {
      o->onError(status);
    }
    return;
  }

  WiFi.config(ipParsed, gatewayParsed, subnetParsed, dns1, dns2);
  connect(connectCmd.ssid, connectCmd.password);
}

void EspWifiSTA::connect(const std::string &ssid, const std::string &pass) {
  if (isConnected()) {
    disconnect();
  }

  WiFi.begin(ssid.c_str(), pass.c_str());

  int tries = 0;
  while (!isConnected() && tries < 50) {
    wl_status_t status = WiFi.status();
    if (status == WL_NO_SSID_AVAIL || status == WL_CONNECT_FAILED) {
      break;
    }
    vTaskDelay(pdMS_TO_TICKS(200));
    tries++;
  }
  if (isConnected()) {
    WiFi.setAutoReconnect(true);
    vTaskDelay(pdMS_TO_TICKS(200));
  } else {
    const WifiScanDto status = setErrorStatus();
    for (auto *o : lockedObservers()) {
      o->onError(status);
    }
  }
}

void EspWifiSTA::disconnect() { WiFi.disconnect(); }

bool EspWifiSTA::isConnected() { return WiFi.isConnected(); }

bool EspWifiSTA::isScanning() { return scanning; }

void EspWifiSTA::startScan() {
  // The scan task picks this up and runs blocking scans while it is set.
  scanning = true;
}

void EspWifiSTA::stopScan() {
  if (!scanning.exchange(false)) {
    return;
  }
  // Aborts a blocking scan in progress, if any; the scan task discards
  // the partial result because `scanning` is already false.
  esp_wifi_scan_stop();
  std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(scanMutex);
  WiFi.scanDelete();
}

void EspWifiSTA::registerObserver(WifiSTAObserver *observer) {
  std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(observersMutex);
  observers.push_back(observer);
}

void EspWifiSTA::unregisterObserver(WifiSTAObserver *observer) {
  std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(observersMutex);
  observers.erase(std::remove(observers.begin(), observers.end(), observer),
                  observers.end());
}

std::vector<WifiSTAObserver *> EspWifiSTA::lockedObservers() {
  std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(observersMutex);
  return observers;
}

WifiScanDto EspWifiSTA::snapshotStatus() {
  std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(statusMutex);
  return statusNetwork;
}

WifiScanDto EspWifiSTA::setErrorStatus() {
  std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(statusMutex);
  statusNetwork.status = WifiStatus::WIFI_ERROR;
  return statusNetwork;
}

void EspWifiSTA::processScan() {
  if (!scanning) {
    return;
  }

  // Blocking all-channel scan: guarantees a complete result list. The old
  // async scan + poll flow could time out (6s in the Arduino core) and
  // restart the scan mid-flight, which made the Wi-Fi driver report only
  // the partial results of the overridden scan (one or two networks per
  // event). This task exists precisely to block here.
  // 120ms per channel keeps the off-channel bursts short so the SoftAP
  // keeps its beacons flowing while a client is browsing the portal.
  const int n = WiFi.scanNetworks(false, true, false, 120);
  if (n < 0) {
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(scanMutex);
    WiFi.scanDelete();
    return;
  }

  std::vector<WifiScanDto> networks;
  {
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(scanMutex);
    // stopScan() may have run while we were waiting for the scan.
    if (!scanning) {
      WiFi.scanDelete();
      return;
    }

    std::string connectedSsid;
    {
      std::lock_guard<std::mutex> statusLock(statusMutex);
      connectedSsid = statusNetwork.ssid;
    }

    for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) {
      auto net = processScanItem(i);
      // Hide the network being connected/connected to, but keep hidden
      // networks (empty ssid) in the list when nothing is being connected.
      if (!connectedSsid.empty() && net.ssid == connectedSsid) {
        continue;
      }
      networks.push_back(std::move(net));
    }

    WiFi.scanDelete();
  }

  for (auto *o : lockedObservers()) {
    o->onScan(networks);
  }
}

WifiScanDto EspWifiSTA::processScanItem(int i) {
  WifiScanDto net;
  net.ssid = std::string(WiFi.SSID(i).c_str());
  net.secure = (WiFi.encryptionType(i) != WIFI_AUTH_OPEN);
  net.status = WifiStatus::WIFI_NONE;
  net.rssi = WiFi.RSSI(i);
  return net;
}

void EspWifiSTA::onWifiEvent(WiFiEvent_t event, WiFiEventInfo_t info) {
  switch (event) {
  case ARDUINO_EVENT_WIFI_STA_GOT_IP: {
    WifiScanDto status;
    {
      std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(statusMutex);
      statusNetwork.status = WifiStatus::WIFI_CONNECTED;
      statusNetwork.rssi = WiFi.RSSI();
      status = statusNetwork;
    }
    for (auto *o : lockedObservers()) {
      o->onConnect(status);
    }
    break;
  }
  case ARDUINO_EVENT_WIFI_STA_DISCONNECTED: {
    WifiScanDto status;
    {
      std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(statusMutex);
      if (statusNetwork.status == WifiStatus::WIFI_CONNECTING) {
        // The connect() loop reports the failure itself.
        break;
      }
      status = statusNetwork;
      statusNetwork.status = WifiStatus::WIFI_NONE;
      statusNetwork.ssid.clear();
      statusNetwork.secure = false;
      statusNetwork.rssi = 0;
    }
    if (status.status == WifiStatus::WIFI_CONNECTED) {
      for (auto *o : lockedObservers()) {
        o->onDisconnect(status);
      }
    }
    break;
  }
  default:
    break;
  }
}

                deviceId.c_str());

void DeviceRegisterLogger::onRejected(const std::string &message,
                                      bool preProvisioned) {
  Serial.printf("[Register] registration REJECTED: %s\n", message.c_str());
  if (preProvisioned) {
    Serial.println("[Register] CRITICAL: the pre-provisioned key conflicts "
                   "with the platform registration for this MAC; fix it on "
                   "the server side (device-api.md section 1)");
  }
}

                                        bool keepingKey) {
  Serial.printf("[Register] %s\n", reason.c_str());
  if (keepingKey) {
    Serial.println("[Register] keeping the pre-provisioned key; the "
                   "platform will confirm on a later connection");
  }
}

  Serial.println("[Register] private.pem saved");
}

void DeviceRegisterLogger::onKeySaveFailed() {
  Serial.println("[Register] CRITICAL: registered but private.pem could "
                 "not be saved");
}

  Serial.printf("[Register] platform access token: %s\n", token.c_str());

===== src/infra/console/FileConsole.cpp =====
#include "infra/console/FileConsole.hpp"
#include <Arduino.h>

namespace {
// Two minutes to paste the content after "restore <file>".
constexpr uint32_t kRestoreTimeoutMs = 120000;
} // namespace

FileConsole::FileConsole(FileStorage &storage) : storage(storage) {}

void FileConsole::poll() {
  while (Serial.available() > 0) {
    const char c = static_cast<char>(Serial.read());
    if (c == '\n' || c == '\r') {
      if (!lineBuffer.empty()) {
        handleLine(lineBuffer);
        lineBuffer.clear();
      }
    } else {
      lineBuffer.push_back(c);
    }
  }

  if (restoring && millis() - lastRestoreActivityMs > kRestoreTimeoutMs) {
    Serial.println("[Console] restore timed out, discarded");
    restoring = false;
    restoreBuffer.clear();
  }
}

void FileConsole::handleLine(const std::string &line) {
  if (restoring) {
    lastRestoreActivityMs = millis();
    if (line == ".") {
      finishRestore();
    } else {
      restoreBuffer += line;
      restoreBuffer += '\n';
    }
    return;
  }

  if (line == "help") {
    Serial.println("commands: dump <file> | restore <file>");
    return;
  }
  if (line.rfind("dump ", 0) == 0) {
    dump(line.substr(5));
    return;
  }
  if (line.rfind("restore ", 0) == 0) {
    beginRestore(line.substr(8));
    return;
  }
}

void FileConsole::dump(const std::string &filename) {
  std::string content;
  if (!storage.load(filename.c_str(), content)) {
    Serial.printf("[Console] %s not found\n", filename.c_str());
    return;
  }
  Serial.println("-----BEGIN DUMP-----");
  Serial.print(content.c_str());
  if (content.empty() || content.back() != '\n') {
    Serial.println();
  }
  Serial.println("-----END DUMP-----");
}

void FileConsole::beginRestore(const std::string &filename) {
  restoring = true;
  restoreTarget = filename;
  restoreBuffer.clear();
  lastRestoreActivityMs = millis();
  Serial.printf("[Console] paste the content of %s now, finish with a line "
                "containing only \".\"\n",
                filename.c_str());
}

void FileConsole::finishRestore() {
  restoring = false;
  const bool ok = storage.save(
      restoreTarget.c_str(),
      reinterpret_cast<const uint8_t *>(restoreBuffer.data()),
      restoreBuffer.size());
  if (ok) {
    Serial.printf("[Console] %s restored (%u bytes); reboot to use it\n",
                  restoreTarget.c_str(), (unsigned)restoreBuffer.size());
  } else {
    Serial.printf("[Console] failed to write %s\n", restoreTarget.c_str());
  }
  restoreBuffer.clear();
}

===== src/infra/mqtt/EspMqttClient.cpp =====
#include "infra/mqtt/EspMqttClient.hpp"
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstring>
#include <algorithm>
#include <esp_crt_bundle.h>

namespace {

// esp-mqtt (IDF 4.4) registers the TLS transport only as "mqtts"; a
// "ssl://" URI would resolve to no transport at all, so normalize it.
std::string normalizedUri(std::string uri) {
  if (uri.rfind("ssl://", 0) == 0) {
    uri.replace(0, 6, "mqtts://");
  }
  return uri;
}

// AA:BB:CC:DD:EE:FF -> aa-bb-cc-dd-ee-ff (safe for MQTT client ids)
std::string sanitizedMac(const std::string &mac) {
  std::string out;
  out.reserve(mac.size());
  for (const char c : mac) {
    if (c == ':') {
      out += '-';
    } else {
      out += static_cast<char>(std::tolower(static_cast<unsigned char>(c)));
    }
  }
  return out;
}

} // namespace

EspMqttClient::EspMqttClient(const std::string &uri, const std::string &mac)
    : uri(normalizedUri(uri)),
      clientId(std::string(MQTT_CLIENT_ID) + "-" + sanitizedMac(mac)) {
  connectedSem = xSemaphoreCreateBinary();

  esp_mqtt_client_config_t config = {};
  config.uri = this->uri.c_str();
  config.client_id = this->clientId.c_str();
  config.keepalive = 60;
  config.disable_auto_reconnect = true; // connect()/disconnect() drive it
  // Verify the broker certificate against the bundled Mozilla roots
  // (esp-tls refuses TLS with no verification option at all).
  config.crt_bundle_attach = esp_crt_bundle_attach;
  if (strlen(MQTT_USERNAME) > 0) {
    config.username = MQTT_USERNAME;
  }
  if (strlen(MQTT_PASSWORD) > 0) {
    config.password = MQTT_PASSWORD;
  }
  client = esp_mqtt_client_init(&config);
  if (client == nullptr) {
    return;
  }
  esp_mqtt_client_register_event(client, (esp_mqtt_event_id_t)ESP_EVENT_ANY_ID,
                                 &EspMqttClient::onEvent, this);
}

EspMqttClient::~EspMqttClient() {
  if (client != nullptr) {
    esp_mqtt_client_stop(client);
    esp_mqtt_client_destroy(client);
    client = nullptr;
  }
  if (connectedSem != nullptr) {
    vSemaphoreDelete(connectedSem);
    connectedSem = nullptr;
  }
}

void EspMqttClient::onMessage(MessageHandler handler) {
  this->handler = std::move(handler);
}

void EspMqttClient::registerObserver(MqttObserver *observer) {
  observers.push_back(observer);
}

void EspMqttClient::unregisterObserver(MqttObserver *observer) {
  observers.erase(std::remove(observers.begin(), observers.end(), observer),
                  observers.end());
}

bool EspMqttClient::connect(uint32_t timeoutMs) {
  if (client == nullptr) {
    return false;
  }
  if (started) {
    esp_mqtt_client_stop(client);
    started = false;
  }
  xSemaphoreTake(connectedSem, 0); // drain a stale connection signal

  if (esp_mqtt_client_start(client) != ESP_OK) {
    return false;
  }
  started = true;
  return xSemaphoreTake(connectedSem, pdMS_TO_TICKS(timeoutMs)) == pdTRUE;
}

void EspMqttClient::disconnect() {
  if (client != nullptr && started) {
    esp_mqtt_client_stop(client);
    started = false;
  }
}

bool EspMqttClient::subscribe(const std::string &topic) {
  if (client == nullptr || !started) {
    return false;
  }
  return esp_mqtt_client_subscribe(client, topic.c_str(), MQTT_QOS) != -1;
}

bool EspMqttClient::publish(const std::string &topic,
                            const std::string &payload) {
  if (client == nullptr || !started) {
    return false;
  }
  const int msgId = esp_mqtt_client_publish(client, topic.c_str(),
                                            payload.c_str(), payload.size(),
                                            MQTT_QOS, 0);
  return msgId >= 0;
}

void EspMqttClient::onEvent(void *arg, esp_event_base_t /*base*/, int32_t id,
                            void *data) {
  static_cast<EspMqttClient *>(arg)->handleEvent(
      static_cast<esp_mqtt_event_id_t>(id),
      static_cast<esp_mqtt_event_handle_t>(data));
}

void EspMqttClient::handleEvent(esp_mqtt_event_id_t id,
                                esp_mqtt_event_handle_t event) {
  switch (id) {
  case MQTT_EVENT_CONNECTED:
    xSemaphoreGive(connectedSem);
    for (auto *o : observers) {
      o->onConnected();
    }
    break;
  case MQTT_EVENT_DATA: {
    if (!handler) {
      break;
    }
    // event->data is not null-terminated; event->topic is, but use the
    // length to be safe.
    const std::string topic(event->topic, event->topic_len);
    const std::string payload(event->data, event->data_len);
    handler(topic, payload);
    break;
  }
  case MQTT_EVENT_ERROR: {
    char reason[80] = "connection error";
    if (event->error_handle != nullptr) {
      snprintf(reason, sizeof reason, "type=%d connect_return=%d tls=0x%04x",
               event->error_handle->error_type,
               event->error_handle->connect_return_code,
               event->error_handle->esp_tls_last_esp_err);
    }
    for (auto *o : observers) {
      o->onError(reason);
    }
    break;
  }
  default:
    break;
  }
}

===== src/infra/security/DeviceAuthenticator.cpp =====
#include "infra/security/DeviceAuthenticator.hpp"
#include "application/security/JwtBuilder.hpp"
#include <time.h>

namespace {
// 2020-01-01: below this the clock is not synced and `exp` would be invalid.
constexpr time_t MIN_VALID_TIME = 1577836800;
constexpr long TOKEN_TTL_SECONDS = 300;
} // namespace

DeviceAuthenticator::DeviceAuthenticator(DeviceKeys &keys, std::string mac)
    : keys(keys), mac(std::move(mac)) {}

std::string DeviceAuthenticator::issueToken() {
  if (!keys.isLoaded()) {
    return {};
  }

  const time_t now = time(nullptr);
  if (now < MIN_VALID_TIME) {
    return {};
  }

  // The MAC is hexadecimal with colons, no JSON escaping needed.
  const std::string payloadJson = "{\"iss\":\"echoes-device\",\"mac\":\"" +
                                  mac + "\",\"exp\":" +
                                  std::to_string(now + TOKEN_TTL_SECONDS) +
                                  "}";

  const std::string signingInput =
      JwtBuilder::base64Url(R"({"alg":"ES256","typ":"JWT"})") + "." +
      JwtBuilder::base64Url(payloadJson);

  std::string der;
  if (!keys.sign(signingInput, der)) {
    return {};
  }

  // ES256 JWTs carry the signature as raw R || S (JOSE), not DER.
  const std::string jose = JwtBuilder::derToJose(der);
  if (jose.size() != 64) {
    return {};
  }

  return signingInput + "." + JwtBuilder::base64Url(jose);
}

===== src/infra/security/MbedtlsDeviceKeys.cpp =====
#include "infra/security/MbedtlsDeviceKeys.hpp"
#include <cstring>
#include <esp_system.h>
#include <mbedtls/ecp.h>
#include <mbedtls/sha256.h>

namespace {
constexpr size_t kPemBufferSize = 1600;
} // namespace

int MbedtlsDeviceKeys::rng(void * /*ctx*/, unsigned char *buf, size_t len) {
  esp_fill_random(buf, len);
  return 0;
}

MbedtlsDeviceKeys::MbedtlsDeviceKeys(FileStorage &storage, std::string path)
    : storage(storage), path(std::move(path)) {
  mbedtls_pk_init(&pk);
}

MbedtlsDeviceKeys::~MbedtlsDeviceKeys() { mbedtls_pk_free(&pk); }

bool MbedtlsDeviceKeys::load() {
  std::string pem;
  if (!storage.load(path.c_str(), pem)) {
    return false;
  }

  // mbedtls_pk_parse_key needs a null-terminated buffer.
  pem.push_back('\0');
  {
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex);
    mbedtls_pk_free(&pk);
    mbedtls_pk_init(&pk);
    const int ret =
        mbedtls_pk_parse_key(&pk, reinterpret_cast<const unsigned char *>(
                                      pem.data()),
                              pem.size(), nullptr, 0);
    if (ret != 0) {
      return false;
    }
    loaded = true;
  }
  return true;
}

bool MbedtlsDeviceKeys::create() {
  std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex);
  mbedtls_pk_free(&pk);
  mbedtls_pk_init(&pk);

  int ret = mbedtls_pk_setup(&pk, mbedtls_pk_info_from_type(MBEDTLS_PK_ECKEY));
  if (ret != 0) {
    return false;
  }
  ret = mbedtls_ecp_gen_key(MBEDTLS_ECP_DP_SECP256R1, mbedtls_pk_ec(pk), rng,
                            nullptr);
  if (ret != 0) {
    return false;
  }

  loaded = true;
  return true;
}

bool MbedtlsDeviceKeys::store() {
  std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex);
  unsigned char pem[kPemBufferSize];
  const int ret = mbedtls_pk_write_key_pem(&pk, pem, sizeof(pem));
  if (ret != 0) {
    return false;
  }
  if (!storage.save(path.c_str(), pem, strlen(reinterpret_cast<char *>(pem)))) {
    return false;
  }
  return true;
}

void MbedtlsDeviceKeys::reset() {
  std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex);
  mbedtls_pk_free(&pk);
  mbedtls_pk_init(&pk);
  loaded = false;
}

std::string MbedtlsDeviceKeys::publicKeyPem() {
  std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex);
  if (!loaded) {
    return {};
  }
  unsigned char pem[kPemBufferSize];
  const int ret = mbedtls_pk_write_pubkey_pem(&pk, pem, sizeof(pem));
  if (ret != 0) {
    return {};
  }
  return std::string(reinterpret_cast<char *>(pem));
}

bool MbedtlsDeviceKeys::sign(const std::string &message, std::string &der) {
  der.clear();
  std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex);
  if (!loaded) {
    return false;
  }

  // In this mbedtls the int-returning variants carry the _ret suffix.
  unsigned char hash[32];
  if (mbedtls_sha256_ret(
          reinterpret_cast<const unsigned char *>(message.data()),
          message.size(), hash, 0) != 0) { // 0 = SHA-256 (not SHA-224)
    return false;
  }

  unsigned char
      sig[MBEDTLS_PK_SIGNATURE_MAX_SIZE]; // > MBEDTLS_ECDSA_MAX_LEN
  size_t sigLen = 0;
  const int ret = mbedtls_pk_sign(&pk, MBEDTLS_MD_SHA256, hash, sizeof(hash),
                                  sig, &sigLen, rng, nullptr);
  if (ret != 0) {
    return false;
  }

  der.assign(reinterpret_cast<const char *>(sig), sigLen);
  return true;
}

===== src/infra/storage/LittleFsStorage.cpp =====
#include "infra/storage/LittleFsStorage.hpp"
#include <LittleFS.h>

bool LittleFsStorage::save(const char *filename, const uint8_t *data,
                           size_t size) {
  File file = LittleFS.open(filename, FILE_WRITE);
  if (!file) {
    return false;
  }
  const size_t written = file.write(data, size);
  file.close();
  return written == size;
}

bool LittleFsStorage::load(const char *filename, std::string &out) {
  out.clear();
  if (!LittleFS.exists(filename)) {
    return false;
  }
  File file = LittleFS.open(filename, FILE_READ);
  if (!file) {
    return false;
  }
  out.resize(file.size());
  const size_t read = out.empty()
                          ? 0
                          : file.read(reinterpret_cast<uint8_t *>(&out[0]),
                                      out.size());
  file.close();
  if (read != out.size()) {
    out.clear();
    return false;
  }
  return true;
}

bool LittleFsStorage::exists(const char *filename) {
  return LittleFS.exists(filename);
}

bool LittleFsStorage::remove(const char *filename) {
  return LittleFS.remove(filename);
}

===== src/infra/time/SntpClock.cpp =====
#include "infra/time/SntpClock.hpp"
#include <Arduino.h>

void SntpClock::sync() {
  // Idempotent: restarts the SNTP request with the current servers.
  configTzTime("UTC", "pool.ntp.org", "time.google.com", "time.nist.gov");
}

===== src/web/SetupController.cpp =====
#include "web/SetupController.hpp"
#include "application/hardware/dto/WifiConnectDto.hpp"
#include <ArduinoJson.h>
#include <LittleFS.h>

SetupController::~SetupController() { delete setupHandler; }

void SetupController::setup() {
  setupHandler = new AsyncCallbackJsonWebHandler(
      "/wifi-setup", [this](AsyncWebServerRequest *request, JsonVariant &json) {
        this->handleSetup(request, json);
      });
  setupHandler->setMethod(HTTP_POST);
  setupHandler->setMaxContentLength(2048);
  server.addHandler(setupHandler);
  server.addHandler(&events);
  server.on("/wifi-list", HTTP_GET, [this](AsyncWebServerRequest *request) {
    this->handleScanList(request);
  });
  server.onNotFound(
      [this](AsyncWebServerRequest *request) { this->handleFile(request); });
}

void SetupController::start() {
  if (running) {
    return;
  }
  server.begin();
  running = true;
}

void SetupController::stop() {
  if (!running) {
    return;
  }
  server.end();
  running = false;
}

void SetupController::handleSetup(AsyncWebServerRequest *request,
                                  JsonVariant &json) {
  JsonObject obj = json.as<JsonObject>();
  // Missing keys must become empty strings. A plain `String x = obj["key"]`
  // serializes a null JsonVariant to the literal "null", which would make
  // isDHCP() false and send a DHCP request down the static-IP path.
  String ssid = obj["ssid"] | "";
  String password = obj["password"] | "";
  String ip = obj["ip"] | "";
  String gateway = obj["gateway"] | "";
  String subnet = obj["subnet"] | "";

  if (ssid.isEmpty()) {
    request->send(400, "text/plain", "Missing ssid");
    return;
  }

  WifiConnectDto dto{ssid.c_str(), password.c_str(), ip.c_str(),
                     gateway.c_str(), subnet.c_str()};

  wifiSTA.connect(dto);

  auto *response = request->beginResponse(200);
  response->addHeader("Cache-Control", "no-store");
  response->addHeader("Connection", "close");
  request->send(response);
}

void SetupController::handleScanList(AsyncWebServerRequest *request) {
  String json;
  {
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(scanMutex);
    json = serializeScanList(lastScan);
  }
  auto *response = request->beginResponse(200, "application/json", json);
  response->addHeader("Cache-Control", "no-store");
  request->send(response);
}

void SetupController::handleFile(AsyncWebServerRequest *request) {
  String path = request->url();

  if (path == "/")
    path = "/index.html";

  // Only web assets are served. Anything else — captive portal probes and
  // device files such as /private.pem — falls back to the app page, never
  // to the filesystem.
  const bool servable = path == "/index.html" || path == "/icon.svg" ||
                        path.startsWith("/styles/") ||
                        path.startsWith("/scripts/");
  if (!servable || !LittleFS.exists(path))
    path = "/index.html";

  String contentType = getContentType(path);

  AsyncWebServerResponse *response =
      request->beginResponse(LittleFS, path, contentType);

  response->addHeader("Cache-Control", "no-store");
  response->addHeader("Connection", "close");

  request->send(response);
}

String SetupController::getContentType(String filename) {
  if (filename.endsWith(".html"))
    return "text/html";
  if (filename.endsWith(".css"))
    return "text/css";
  if (filename.endsWith(".js"))
    return "application/javascript";
  if (filename.endsWith(".png"))
    return "image/png";
  if (filename.endsWith(".jpg"))
    return "image/jpeg";
  if (filename.endsWith(".svg"))
    return "image/svg+xml";
  if (filename.endsWith(".ico"))
    return "image/x-icon";
  return "text/plain";
}

String SetupController::serializeScanList(
    const std::vector<WifiScanDto> &scanList) const {
  JsonDocument doc;
  JsonArray array = doc.to<JsonArray>();
  for (auto &network : scanList) {
    JsonObject o = array.add<JsonObject>();
    o["ssid"] = network.ssid;
    o["secure"] = network.secure;
    o["rssi"] = network.rssi;
    o["status"] = static_cast<int>(network.status);
  }
  String json;
  serializeJson(doc, json);
  return json;
}

void SetupController::onConnect(const WifiScanDto &connectDto) {
  updateStatus(connectDto, WifiStatus::WIFI_CONNECTED);
}

void SetupController::onConnecting(const WifiScanDto &connectDto) {
  updateStatus(connectDto, WifiStatus::WIFI_CONNECTING);
}

void SetupController::onError(const WifiScanDto &connectDto) {
  updateStatus(connectDto, WifiStatus::WIFI_ERROR);
}

void SetupController::onDisconnect(const WifiScanDto &connectDto) {
  updateStatus(connectDto, WifiStatus::WIFI_NONE);
}

void SetupController::updateStatus(const WifiScanDto &connectDto,
                                   WifiStatus status) {
  JsonDocument doc;
  doc["ssid"] = connectDto.ssid;
  doc["status"] = static_cast<int>(status);
  doc["rssi"] = connectDto.rssi;
  doc["secure"] = connectDto.secure;
  String json;
  serializeJson(doc, json);
  events.send(json, "status");
}

void SetupController::onScan(const std::vector<WifiScanDto> &scanList) {
  {
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(scanMutex);
    lastScan = scanList;
  }
  events.send(serializeScanList(scanList), "list");
}

===== src/main.cpp =====
#include "application/service/DeviceRegister.hpp"
#include "application/service/ScanService.hpp"
#include "infra/console/FileConsole.hpp"
#include "infra/hardware/EspMacAddress.hpp"
#include "infra/hardware/EspWifiAP.hpp"
#include "infra/hardware/EspWifiSTA.hpp"
#include "infra/hardware/WifiIndicator.hpp"
#include "infra/hardware/WifiSTAProxy.hpp"
#include "infra/logging/DeviceRegisterLogger.hpp"
#include "infra/logging/MqttLogger.hpp"
#include "infra/logging/WifiAPLogger.hpp"
#include "infra/logging/WifiSTALogger.hpp"
#include "infra/mqtt/EspMqttClient.hpp"
#include "infra/security/DeviceAuthenticator.hpp"
#include "infra/security/MbedtlsDeviceKeys.hpp"
#include "infra/storage/LittleFsStorage.hpp"
#include "infra/time/SntpClock.hpp"
#include "web/SetupController.hpp"
#include <Arduino.h>
#include <LittleFS.h>
#include <nvs_flash.h>

extern "C" void app_main(void) {
  esp_err_t err = nvs_flash_init();
  if (err == ESP_ERR_NVS_NO_FREE_PAGES ||
      err == ESP_ERR_NVS_NEW_VERSION_FOUND) {
    ESP_ERROR_CHECK(nvs_flash_erase());
    err = nvs_flash_init();
  }
  ESP_ERROR_CHECK(err);

  initArduino();
  Serial.setRxBufferSize(1024); // room to paste a whole PEM over the console
  Serial.begin(115200);

  EspWifiAP wifiAP;
  EspWifiSTA espWifiSTA;
  WifiSTAProxy wifiSTA{espWifiSTA};
  WifiSTALogger staLogger;
  WifiAPLogger apLogger;
  SetupController setupController{wifiSTA};
  WifiIndicator wifiIndicator;
  ScanService scanService{wifiSTA};

  wifiSTA.setup();
  wifiAP.start();

  const std::string mac = EspMacAddress::get();
  LittleFsStorage storage;
  MbedtlsDeviceKeys deviceKeys{storage, "/private.pem"};
  EspMqttClient mqtt{MQTT_BROKER_URL, mac};
  DeviceAuthenticator authenticator{deviceKeys, mac};
  SntpClock sntpClock;
  DeviceRegister deviceRegister{deviceKeys, mqtt, authenticator,
                                        sntpClock, mac};
  DeviceRegisterLogger deviceRegisterLogger;
  MqttLogger mqttLogger;
  FileConsole fileConsole{storage};

  if (!LittleFS.begin(false)) {
    Serial.println("LittleFS mount failed, formatting...");
    if (!LittleFS.begin(true)) {
      // Keep running: the AP and the setup API still work without the UI.
      Serial.println("LittleFS format failed! Web UI unavailable.");
    }
  }

  wifiSTA.registerObserver(&staLogger);
  wifiSTA.registerObserver(&setupController);
  wifiSTA.registerObserver(&wifiIndicator);
  wifiSTA.registerObserver(&scanService);
  wifiSTA.registerObserver(&deviceRegister);
  wifiAP.registerObserver(&apLogger);
  wifiAP.registerObserver(&scanService);
  wifiAP.registerObserver(&deviceRegister);

  setupController.setup();
  setupController.start();

  scanService.begin();

  mqtt.registerObserver(&mqttLogger);
  deviceRegister.registerObserver(&deviceRegisterLogger);
  deviceRegister.registerObserver(&scanService);
  deviceRegister.begin();

  while (true) {
    fileConsole.poll();
    delay(25);
  }
}

===== test/README =====

This directory is intended for PlatformIO Test Runner and project tests.

Unit Testing is a software testing method by which individual units of
source code, sets of one or more MCU program modules together with associated
control data, usage procedures, and operating procedures, are tested to
determine whether they are fit for use. Unit testing finds problems early
in the development cycle.

More information about PlatformIO Unit Testing:
- https://docs.platformio.org/en/latest/advanced/unit-testing/index.html

===== data/scripts/main.js =====
import { WifiRender } from "./view/WifiRender.js";
import { WifiManager } from "./manager/WifiManager.js";

document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
const wifiManager = new WifiManager();
const wifiRender = new WifiRender(wifiManager);

wifiManager.addListener('fetch', () => wifiRender.render());
wifiManager.addListener('update', () => wifiRender.render());
wifiManager.setup();
wifiRender.setup();

})

===== data/scripts/manager/WifiManager.js =====
export class WifiManager {
  wifis = [];
  status = null;
  #listeners = {
    fetch: [],
    add: [],
    update: [],
  };
  #evt;

  constructor() {
    this.#evt = new EventSource('/events');
  }

  setup() {
    // First load only: fetch the last scan list so the page renders right
    // away, before the first SSE event arrives. Updates come via SSE.
    this.#fetchWifiList();
    this.#evt.addEventListener('status', e => this.#fetchStatus(e));
    this.#evt.addEventListener('list', e => this.#fetchWifi(e));
  }

  setWifis(wifis) {
    this.wifis = wifis;
    this.#notify('fetch');
  }

  async #fetchWifiList() {
    try {
      const response = await fetch('/wifi-list');
      if (!response.ok)
        return;
      this.setWifis(await response.json());
    } catch (err) {
      console.error(err);
    }
  }

  async #fetchWifi(evt) {
    const data = JSON.parse(evt.data);
    this.wifis = data;
    this.#notify('fetch');
  }

  async #fetchStatus(evt) {
    const data = JSON.parse(evt.data);
    if (!data.ssid)
      return;

    if (data.status === 0) {
      // Disconnected: drop the status card, the network returns to the list.
      if (this.status && this.status.ssid === data.ssid)
        this.status = null;
      this.#notify('update');
      return;
    }

    const networkIndex = this.wifis.findIndex(w => w.ssid === data.ssid);
    let wifi = data;
    if (networkIndex !== -1) {
      const known = this.wifis[networkIndex];
      // Keep signal/lock info from the scan while there is no real value.
      wifi = {
        ...data,
        rssi: data.rssi || known.rssi,
        secure: data.secure || known.secure,
      };
      this.wifis.splice(networkIndex, 1);
    }
    this.status = wifi;
    this.#notify('update');
  }

  async connectToWifi(wifiConnectDto) {
    try {
      await this.#_connectToWifi(wifiConnectDto);
      this.#markStatus(wifiConnectDto, 1);
    } catch (err) {
      console.error(err);
      this.#markStatus(wifiConnectDto, 3);
      throw new Error('Erro ao conectar');
    }
  }

  async #_connectToWifi(wifiDto) {
    const response = await fetch('/wifi-setup', {
      method: "POST",
      headers: {
        'Content-Type': 'application/json',
      },
      body: JSON.stringify(wifiDto),
    });
    if (!response.ok)
      throw new Error('Erro ao conectar');
  }

  #markStatus(wifiDto, status) {
    if (this.status && this.status.ssid !== wifiDto.ssid) {
      // The previous status card goes back to the list.
      const previous = { ...this.status, status: 0 };
      if (!this.wifis.some(w => w.ssid === previous.ssid))
        this.wifis.unshift(previous);
      this.status = null;
    }
    const i = this.wifis.findIndex(w => w.ssid === wifiDto.ssid);
    if (i >= 0) {
      this.wifis[i].status = status;
      this.#notify('update');
      return;
    }

    const wifi = {
      ssid: wifiDto.ssid,
      status: status,
      secure: wifiDto.password !== '',
      rssi: 0,
    };
    this.status = wifi;
    this.#notify('update');
  }

  addListener(event, cb) {
    if (!this.#listeners[event])
      return;
    this.#listeners[event].push(cb);
  }

  removeListener(event, cb) {
    if (!this.#listeners[event])
      return;
    const i = this.#listeners[event].findIndex(i => i === cb);
    if (i >= 0)
      this.#listeners[event].splice(i, 1);
  }

  #notify(event) {
    if (!this.#listeners[event])
      return;
    for (const cb of this.#listeners[event]) {
      cb();
    }
  }
}

    this.status = status;
    this.rssi = rssi;
  }

===== data/scripts/util/Debouncer.js =====
export class Debouncer {
  #input = null;
  #timer = null;
  #callbacks = [];
  #interval = 500;

  constructor(selector, event) {
    this.#input = document.querySelector(selector);
    if (this.#input)
      this.#input.addEventListener(event, ev => this.#debounce(ev));
  }

  addCallback(cb) {
    this.#callbacks.push(cb);
  }

  removeCallback(cb) {
    const i = this.#callbacks.indexOf(cb);
    if (i !== -1)
      this.#callbacks.splice(i, 1);
  }

  #debounce(ev) {
    clearTimeout(this.#timer);
    this.#timer = setTimeout(() => {
      this.#notify(ev);
    }, this.#interval);
  }

  #notify(ev) {
    for (const cb of this.#callbacks) {
      cb(ev);
    }
  }
}

===== data/scripts/view/WifiForm.js =====
import { Debouncer } from "../util/Debouncer.js";
import { WifiConnectDto } from "../manager/dto/WifiConnectDto.js";

export class WifiForm {
  #wifiManager = null;
  #wifiIpDebouncer = null;
  #wifiModal = null;
  #wifiModalCancel = null;
  #wifiConnect = null;
  #wifiSubnet = null;
  #wifiError = null;
  #wifiGateway = null;

  constructor(wifiManager) {
    this.#wifiManager = wifiManager;
    this.#wifiModal = document.getElementById('wifi-modal');
    this.#wifiModalCancel = document.getElementById('wifi-modal-cancel');
    this.#wifiConnect = document.getElementById('wifi-connect');
    this.#wifiSubnet = document.querySelector('input[name=subnet]');
    this.#wifiError = document.getElementById('wifi-error');
    this.#wifiGateway = document.querySelector('input[name=gateway]');
  }

  setup() {
    this.#setupDebouncer();
    this.#setupClickListeners();
    this.#setupConnectRequest();
  }

  #setupDebouncer() {
    this.#wifiIpDebouncer = new Debouncer('input[name=ip]', 'input');
    this.#wifiIpDebouncer.addCallback((ev) => {
      this.#setGatewayFromIp(ev.target.value);
    })
  }

  #setGatewayFromIp(ip) {
    const isValid = this.#isValidIPv4(ip);
    if (!isValid)
      return;

    const numbers = ip.split('.');
    numbers[3] = '1';

    this.#wifiGateway.value = numbers.join('.');
  }

  #isValidIPv4(ip) {
    const regex = /^(25[0-5]|2[0-4]\d|1\d{2}|[1-9]?\d)(\.(25[0-5]|2[0-4]\d|1\d{2}|[1-9]?\d)){3}$/;
    return regex.test(ip);
  }

  #setupClickListeners() {
    this.#wifiModal.addEventListener('click', ev => {
      if (ev.target !== this.#wifiModal)
        return;
      this.#closeModal();
    })
    this.#wifiModalCancel.addEventListener('click', () => {
      this.#closeModal();
    })
  }

  #resetForm() {
    this.#wifiConnect.reset();
    this.#wifiSubnet.value = '255.255.255.0';
  }

  #closeModal() {
    this.#wifiModal.style.display = 'none';
    this.#resetForm();
  }

  clearError() {
    this.#wifiError.textContent = '';
  }

  #setupConnectRequest() {
    this.#wifiConnect.addEventListener('submit', ev => {
      ev.preventDefault();
      this.#requestWifiConnection();
    })
  }

  async #requestWifiConnection() {
    this.clearError();
    try {
      await this.#_requestWifiConnection();
      // Only close the modal when the request was accepted, otherwise the
      // error message would be written to a hidden element.
      this.#closeModal();
    } catch(err) {
      this.#wifiError.textContent = err.message;
    }
  }

  async #_requestWifiConnection() {
    const formData = new FormData(this.#wifiConnect);
    const ssid = formData.get('ssid');
    const password = formData.get('password');
    const ip = formData.get('ip');
    const gateway = formData.get('gateway');
    const subnet = formData.get('subnet');

    if (!ssid)
      throw new Error('Digite o SSID da rede');
    if (ip)
      this.#validateAdvanced(ip, gateway, subnet);

    const wifiConnectDto = new WifiConnectDto(ssid, password, ip, gateway, subnet);
    await this.#wifiManager.connectToWifi(wifiConnectDto);
  }

  #validateAdvanced(ip, gateway, subnet) {
    if (!this.#isValidIPv4(ip))
      throw new Error('Digite um ip válido');
    if (!this.#isValidIPv4(gateway))
      throw new Error('Digite um gateway válido');
    if (!this.#isValidIPv4(subnet))
      throw new Error('Digite uma máscara válida');
  }
}

===== data/scripts/view/WifiRender.js =====
import { WifiForm } from "./WifiForm.js";

export class WifiRender {
  #wifiManager = null;
  #wifiForm = null;
  #networksDiv = null;
  #wifiModal = null;
  #wifiModalName = null;
  #wifiModalSsid = null;
  #wifiModalPassword = null;
  #icons = null;

  constructor(wifiManager) {
    this.#wifiManager = wifiManager;
    this.#wifiForm = new WifiForm(wifiManager);
    this.#networksDiv = document.getElementById('networks');
    this.#wifiModal = document.getElementById('wifi-modal');
    this.#wifiModalName = document.getElementById('wifi-modal-name');
    this.#wifiModalSsid = document.getElementById('wifi-modal-ssid');
    this.#wifiModalPassword = document.getElementById('wifi-modal-password');
  }

  setup() {
    this.#setupIcons()
    this.#wifiForm.setup();
  }

  #setupIcons() {
    const wifiFullIcon = `
      <svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="20" height="20" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="feather feather-wifi"><path d="M5 12.55a11 11 0 0 1 14.08 0"></path><path d="M1.42 9a16 16 0 0 1 21.16 0"></path><path d="M8.53 16.11a6 6 0 0 1 6.95 0"></path><line x1="12" y1="20" x2="12.01" y2="20"></line></svg>
    `;
    const wifi75Icon = `
      <svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="20" height="20" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="feather feather-wifi" style="--darkreader-inline-stroke: currentColor;" data-darkreader-inline-stroke=""><path d="M5 12.55a11 11 0 0 1 14.08 0"></path><path d="M8.53 16.11a6 6 0 0 1 6.95 0"></path><line x1="12" y1="20" x2="12.01" y2="20"></line></svg>
    `;
    const wifi50Icon = `
      <svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="20" height="20" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="feather feather-wifi" style="--darkreader-inline-stroke: currentColor;" data-darkreader-inline-stroke=""><path d="M8.53 16.11a6 6 0 0 1 6.95 0"></path><line x1="12" y1="20" x2="12.01" y2="20"></line></svg>
    `;
    const wifi25Icon = `
      <svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="20" height="20" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="feather feather-wifi" style="--darkreader-inline-stroke: currentColor;" data-darkreader-inline-stroke=""><line x1="12" y1="20" x2="12.01" y2="20"></line></svg>
    `;
    const lockIcon = `
      <svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="20" height="20" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="feather feather-lock"><rect x="3" y="11" width="18" height="11" rx="2" ry="2"></rect><path d="M7 11V7a5 5 0 0 1 10 0v4"></path></svg>
    `;
    const parser = new DOMParser();
    const wifiFullSvg = parser.parseFromString(wifiFullIcon, 'image/svg+xml');
    const wifiFullSvgEl = wifiFullSvg.documentElement;
    const wifi75Svg = parser.parseFromString(wifi75Icon, 'image/svg+xml');
    const wifi75SvgEl = wifi75Svg.documentElement;
    const wifi50Svg = parser.parseFromString(wifi50Icon, 'image/svg+xml');
    const wifi50SvgEl = wifi50Svg.documentElement;
    const wifi25Svg = parser.parseFromString(wifi25Icon, 'image/svg+xml');
    const wifi25SvgEl = wifi25Svg.documentElement;
    const lockSvg = parser.parseFromString(lockIcon, 'image/svg+xml');
    const lockSvgEl = lockSvg.documentElement;
    this.#icons = {
      wifiFull: wifiFullSvgEl,
      wifi75: wifi75SvgEl,
      wifi50: wifi50SvgEl,
      wifi25: wifi25SvgEl,
      lock: lockSvgEl,
    };
  }

  render() {
    this.#networksDiv.innerHTML = '';
    if (this.#wifiManager.status)
      this.renderWifi(this.#wifiManager.status);
    this.#wifiManager.wifis.forEach(wifi => this.renderWifi(wifi));
  }

  renderWifi(wifi) {
    const btn = this.createButton(wifi);

    const div = document.createElement('div');
    if (wifi.rssi >= -55)
      div.appendChild(this.#icons.wifiFull.cloneNode(true));
    else if (wifi.rssi >= -65)
      div.appendChild(this.#icons.wifi75.cloneNode(true));
    else if (wifi.rssi >= -75)
      div.appendChild(this.#icons.wifi50.cloneNode(true));
    else
      div.appendChild(this.#icons.wifi25.cloneNode(true));

    const subDiv = document.createElement('div');
    subDiv.classList.add('network-status');

    const nameSpan = this.createNameSpan(wifi.ssid);
    const statusSpan = this.createStatusSpan(wifi.status);

    subDiv.appendChild(nameSpan);
    subDiv.appendChild(statusSpan);

    div.appendChild(subDiv);
    
    btn.appendChild(div);
    if (wifi.secure)
      btn.appendChild(this.#icons.lock.cloneNode(true));

    this.#networksDiv.appendChild(btn);
  }

  createButton(wifi) {
    const btn = document.createElement('button');
    btn.classList.add('network-option');
    if (wifi.status != 2)
      btn.addEventListener('click', () => this.selectWifi(wifi));
    if (wifi.status == 2)
      btn.style.cursor = 'auto';
    return btn;
  }

  createNameSpan(ssid) {
    const nameSpan = document.createElement('span');
    nameSpan.classList.add('network-name');
    if (ssid === '') {
      nameSpan.textContent = 'Rede oculta';
    } else {
      nameSpan.textContent = ssid;
    }
    return nameSpan;
  }

  createStatusSpan(status) {
    const statusSpan = document.createElement('span');
    switch (status) {
      case 0:
        statusSpan.innerHTML = '&nbsp;';
        break;
      case 1:
        statusSpan.textContent = 'Conectando';
        break;
      case 2:
        statusSpan.textContent = 'Conectado';
        break;
      case 3:
        statusSpan.textContent = 'Erro ao conectar';
        break;
    }
    return statusSpan;
  }

  selectWifi(wifi) {
    this.#wifiForm.clearError();
    this.#wifiModalSsid.type = 'hidden';
    this.#wifiModal.style.display = 'flex';
    this.#wifiModalName.textContent = wifi.ssid;
    this.#wifiModalSsid.value = wifi.ssid;
    if (wifi.ssid === '') {
      this.#wifiModalName.textContent = 'Rede oculta';
      this.#wifiModalSsid.type = 'text';
    }
    if (wifi.secure) {
      this.#wifiModalPassword.style.display = 'flex';
    } else {
      this.#wifiModalPassword.style.display = 'none';
    }
  }
}
